### N. Geometry / volume-profile / candle / liquidity columns (scanner_geometry.py) — EVERY comparison. **Total comparisons in the BH family: 692** (filter tests: x_mkt = excess vs SPY (stocks) / BTC (crypto) under the active stop policy, close-based; with = feature present / quintile / rank top half; level tests: paired per-trade difference vs the active policy on covered trades, mean_with/without = variant/policy raw return). p = max(ticker-clustered, entry-date-clustered, two-way clustered); q = Benjamini-Hochberg over all rows; wd = difference after demeaning x_mkt within entry-date cohorts; half1/half2 = difference in the earlier/later half of entry dates; p_incr = p of the feature after controlling for the existing rule inputs (20-method score, % vs SMA200, sector uptrend).
